- **lisa-verify-prd**: Initiative-level PRD acceptance gate. - **lisi-integration-metric-computation**: Use when comparing single-cell batch integration methods on multi-sample, multi-condition data (e.g. pancreas + lung mixtures) and you need quantitative evidence of batch removal (iLISI) without over-correction (cLISI, condition separation, condition-specific DE genes). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/lisi-integration-metric-computation))

- **lisi-integration-quality-metric-computation**: Use when two or more scRNA-seq datasets (e.g., Muraro CEL-Seq2 and Segerstolpe Smart-Seq2) have been integrated by batch-correction tools and you need numeric iLISI/cLISI scores, plus runtime, to rank the methods. - **lisi-metric-computation-on-embeddings**: Use when quantifying batch effects in scRNA-seq data via local batch-label diversity: you have an embedding (BEENE or PCA), per-cell batch labels across multiple batches/platforms, and need per-sample iLISI values for downstream kBET/iLISI comparison tables. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/lisi-metric-computation-on-embeddings))
- **lisi-mixing-metric-computation**: Use when per-cell UMAP embeddings from one or more integration methods (e.g. Supervised Harmony, Harmony, Seurat RPCA, Scanorama, Limma) must be benchmarked for batch/donor mixing versus AUCell-derived cell-type conservation.
